Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on satellites structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 327. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 34 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_327(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 327. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^34(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)
